# EXP-010 results summary

Generated by make_summary.py from artifacts/results.json; every number below is computed, none is typed.
Hypothesis: hypothesis.md (frozen). Counts and members only: no statement of realizability is made here.

Notation: a stratum is S(m; i, j) with f on the monomials of weight class i and g on class j modulo m. r = 2 u(2e) - 2. 'Examined' counts the members whose rank of mu_3e was computed (all members when 'all'; the first members of the enumeration when 'in order, stopped early', which the early stop cut; random members when 'sampled'); 'rank r' those with that rank; 'pure Hilbert function' those that also pass the staged test; 'tables' the Betti tables computed by uhlib; 'pure' those whose table is (1, 2, 2, 1) on (0, e, 3e, 4e). A stratum stops once it has 5 pure members (chunks already started finish and are counted).
